Original minimax Goal OPEN.\n\nRetain the full free-pi notation of the current original-array architecture: Ltri lower triangular, t=Ltri e, h=H_m, n=2^m-1, l=n-1, s=product_(i<n)(h_i-1)>0, q=17/(16D), pi=sqrt(q), E=pi+s, F=pi-s, eta=F/E. Put b=1+a, g=1+beta, and define the unit-curvature PREFIX column\n\n w_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j), so Ltri w=e and w_n=-s.\n\nThe signed occupation weights\n\n p_i=h_i*w_i/(1+s), i<n,\n\nsum to1 by telescoping w_i-w_(i+1)=h_i*w_i. They are signed, not probabilities. Write\n\n Tquad=sum_(i<n)h_i*w_i^2,\n Mclock=sum_(i<n)t_i*h_i*w_i,\n p0=b/(1+s).\n\nDefine a zero-sum column with zero ORIGINAL source coordinate by\n\n u=p-p0*e0-(1-p0)*e1.\n\nThen e^T u=0, u_0=0, and the two exact scalar products should be rederived before use:\n\n w^T u=(Tquad-b^2)/(1+s)+a,\n t^T u=Mclock/(1+s)-(1+b)+b^2/(1+s).\n\nLet r=l-1, the actual penultimate query. Its clock gap to the last query is t_l-t_r=g. Set\n\n uhat=u-[(t^T u)/g]*(e_l-e_r).\n\nThis leaves e^T uhat=0 and uhat_0=0, and makes t^T uhat=0 exactly. Since w_l=s/a and w_r=-s/(a*beta),\n\n w^T uhat=(Tquad-b^2)/(1+s)+a-[s/(a*beta)]*(t^T u).\n\nThus imposing the clock condition at the two last original queries changes the quadratic-prefix product only by a factor containing the extremely small full product s. It does NOT introduce a physical step or change any gradient trajectory.\n\nStarting from the old source-optimal column zsrc=(E/a)(b e0-e1), consider the ONE explicit new column\n\n gamma=q*(1+s)/(2*(q-s^2)),\n znew=zsrc+E*gamma*uhat.\n\nThe denominator is positive if s<pi. This candidate preserves EXACTLY\n\n e^T znew=E,\n t^T znew=-E/a,\n (znew)_0=E*b/a.\n\nConsequently the original Xreq=D-pi^(-2)-eta*(t^T z)^2, source charge calibration, source capacity and source row-clock moment formulas should remain unchanged, provided the complete original polynomial and skew moments are recomputed with this znew. The displayed gamma makes the Tquad contribution of -4F*znew^T w exactly-2q*Tquad, because F*E=q-s^2. This is an algebraic cancellation lead; all tail-pool corrections and all other terms still require a uniform exact bound. The sign of Tw has NOT been established in this advisory.\n\nRemaining obligations before this can be a candidate certificate include:\n\n- Re-derive the full original polynomial, Ae/At compatibility, optimum signs, and source formulas with znew; do not rely on the old sparse common column where it no longer applies.\n- Prove explicit bounds on Mclock and on the added last-two coordinates, keeping the SAME enclosing m. One possible analytic bound uses |w_i|<=beta^i, h_i and t_i bounded by fixed powers of i+1, but every constant/threshold must be supplied.\n- Re-evaluate the full squared-clock cost as well as Tw, all earlier terminal-six cuts, and the true endpoint capacities. Large signed common coordinates are not free merely because the x0 square charge stays q.\n- Choose an explicit PSD metric and a genuinely nonlocal skew completion, and check every ORIGINAL finite-pair sign. Old local skew fixes are already obstructed. Objective legality, energy, and the same-ray estimate\n\nBecause `B >= kappa` and `v >= 0`, the positive-ray law is exactly\n\n    G(r) = kappa*r       for 0 <= r <= 1,\n           r-v           for 1 <= r <= u,\n           B             for r >= u.\n\nThe adjacent expressions agree at 1 and `u`; if `B=kappa`, the middle interval is empty. Since `kappa>0` and `B>=kappa>0`, `G(r)>0` for `r>0`; 0 is its unique minimizer.\n\nDirect integration gives\n\n    2F(r) = kappa*r^2                              for r <= 1,\n            (r-v)^2 + kappa*v                      for 1 <= r <= u,\n            2B*r - B^2 - v*(2B-kappa)              for r >= u,\n\nwhere `F(r)=f(r)`. At the actual `m>=1536`, this gives `d_(m-1)>9`, `S>1000`, and `d_m>2/a`. Exact one-step interval image and crossing price\n\nFor `h=1+d`, the positive-source signed output is\n\n    (1-h*kappa)*r        in the core,\n    h*v-d*r             in the middle,\n    r-h*B               in the tail.\n\nThe exact image radius of `[-R,R]` is\n\n    E_h(R) = max( |1-h*kappa|*min(R,1),\n                  [d*min(R,u)-h*v]_+,\n                  [R-h*B]_+ ).\n\nOn each present affine interval, extrema of the absolute value occur at endpoints. Thus the base gives even `aR<aw`.\n\nFor the parent grid, start at any actual radius at most `c*d_j*w`, write `y` for the original left-child output radius, and split the root bridge:\n\n**Crossing.** If `q=0`, `G(y)<=B=w`. The original left child ends in the core\n\nLet `y=|Psi_J(s)|<=1`. Zero is settled immediately. For positive `y`, put\n\n    d=d_J, L=S_J/d_J, t=kappa*d,\n    q=kappa*(1+d)-1=t-v>0.\n\nThe root reverses and lands at actual radius `z=q*y`, which is permitted to exceed 1. Dimensionless form of the necessary affine test\n\nPut Q=qD, A=pi*sqrt(D), and Bsc=u/sqrt(D). Congruence of the displayed\nmatrix gives\n\n    [[Q-A^2, A*Bsc-1],[A*Bsc-1,1-Bsc^2]].\n\nIts determinant equals\n\n    Q-1-A^2-Q*Bsc^2+2*A*Bsc\n      =(Q-1)*(1-Bsc^2)-(A-Bsc)^2.\n\nFor Q>=1 the PSD conditions can therefore be written as\n\n    A^2<=Q, Bsc^2<=1,\n    (A-Bsc)^2<=(Q-1)*(1-Bsc^2).\n\nAt Q=1 this forces A=Bsc with |A|<=1, that is u=D*pi and pi^2<=1/D.\nAt saturation pi=sqrt(q), the first diagonal is zero, so u=1/pi is\nforced instead. This saturation is a boundary of the affine feasible\nregion, not a necessity proved for the whole certificate cone.\n\nFor Q=17/16 the ellipse width is explicitly\n\n    |A-Bsc| <= (1/4)*sqrt(1-Bsc^2).\n\nIn particular pi=0 is allowed by this necessary screen whenever\nu^2<=D/17. This is not a sufficient dual construction.\n\n## 2. A literal interpretation of pi=0 with last-query terminal contact\n\nSuppose the final physical step is b=1+a in(0,2), l=n-1.
